# Graph Report - VAPS  (2026-08-20)

## Corpus Check
- 1357 files · ~2,580,494 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 19646 nodes · 32883 edges · 1286 communities (1141 shown, 145 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3418 edges (avg confidence: 0.6)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `40095f69`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
